# Skills · G · part 75

978 entries, grilling to grok-imagine-parity. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grilling**: Grill the user relentlessly about a plan, decision, or idea to surface hidden assumptions and lock down tradeoffs. Use when the user wants to stress-test their thinking, uses the '/grill-me' slash command, or when a design has two or more plausible approaches with unresolved tradeoffs. ([gustavo-keiller/pareto-pc-hardware-optimizer](https://github.com/gustavo-keiller/pareto-pc-hardware-optimizer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grilling) · 2 copies)
- **grilling**: Use when a Jira ticket or local request leaves behavior, scope, acceptance criteria, or design choices ambiguous. ([HakjunMIN/ai-native-harness](https://github.com/HakjunMIN/ai-native-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling))
- **grilling**: Interview the owner until a plan or decision is fully understood before writing it. Use before drafting a new plan in docs/plans/, when a requirement is unclear, or when the owner asks to stress-test an idea. ([halilenesozdemir/harfhane](https://github.com/halilenesozdemir/harfhane/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grilling))
- **grilling**: Design-tree interview that stress-tests a plan, decision, or idea in rounds. HX adaptation of mattpocock/skills grilling (MIT): question rounds are capped at 5 per round per owner directive 2026-08-30. NOT the factory scope-lock gate — that is grill-me. Use for design and product stress-testing, typically under grill-with-docs or triage. ([hanax-ai/HX-ASF-Servers](https://github.com/hanax-ai/HX-ASF-Servers/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/grilling) · 3 copies)

- **grilling**: Interview the user relentlessly to stress-test a plan or design before building. Use on any "grill" phrasing. ([henrygoldsmith07-wq/Claude-Code](https://github.com/henrygoldsmith07-wq/Claude-Code/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grilling))

- **grilling**: 用于逐轮追问、盘问或挑刺计划、设计和决策直到达成共识（grill me），以及实施前仍有关键决策未确认时；挑刺代码属于代码评审，不触发。 ([hydrz/dev-skills](https://github.com/hydrz/dev-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling))

- **grilling**: Use when a direction, plan, or design already exists but important product, behavior, scope, API, data, compatibility, or architecture decisions remain unresolved, or when the user asks to stress-test such a proposal. ([IsilAlphare/agent-skills](https://github.com/IsilAlphare/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling))
- **grilling**: Stress-test a plan, decision, or idea by interviewing the user one question at a time, down every branch of the decision tree, until there's a shared understanding worth acting on. Produces agreement, never code. ([itsmechlark/laptop](https://github.com/itsmechlark/laptop/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling))
- **grilling**: Stress-test a consequential or ambiguous plan, decision, or idea through a live interview. OpenSpec 的 proposal、specs、design、tasks 草案已 validate 通过，需要在最终批准和 TDD 前完成压力检验 不触发（留给 brainstorming、openspec、spec-driven-workflow、tdd 或 reviewing-code-changes）： - 用户尚无候选方案，只想探索需求或生成新点子，留给 brainstorming - 用户要求创建、修订或校验正式规格资产，留给 openspec - 用户要求实现功能、修复缺陷或直接修改代码，留给 tdd 或对应执行 Skill - 用户要求评审已经实现的代码变更、PR 或测试覆盖，留给 reviewing-code-changes 默认路由：所有 validate 通过的 OpenSpec 完整 change… ([Vitus213/specpatrol](https://github.com/Vitus213/specpatrol/tree/HEAD/src/specpatrol/skillpack/skills/grilling))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-in-silico-knockout-analysis))
- **grn-inference-benchmarking**: Use when you need to decide how many cells and how many candidate TFs per target are sufficient for reliable scGATE logic-gate inference, or when reproducing runtime/saturation curves like Figure 5C before committing to a full-network run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-inference-benchmarking))
- **grn-inference-benchmarking-with-genie3-and-grnboost**: Use when a workflow depends on a GRN inferred from a single-cell gene expression matrix and you need to test whether the downstream results (e.g., cell-type communication matrices) are robust to swapping the GRN inference algorithm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-inference-benchmarking-with-genie3-and-grnboost))
- **grn-inference-gniplr**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes x cells, .txt, rows = genes, columns = cells) must be converted into a GRN adjacency matrix to serve as the left submatrix R in MDIC3, or when benchmarking GNIPLR against GENIE3/GRNBoost as GRN sources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-inference-gniplr))
- **grn-inference-with-gradient-boosting**: Use when a co-expression module (e.g. a WGCNA module correlated with a trait or cell type) must be converted into a directed TF–target regulatory network to identify the transcription factors controlling it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-inference-with-gradient-boosting))
- **grn-inference-with-grnboost2**: Use when preparing a GRN-guided causal GAN run: you have a preprocessed single-cell expression matrix (e.g., 1000 highly variable genes, library-size normalized to 20,000) and need a causal graph of TF-gene edges to impose during GRouNdGAN training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/grn-inference-with-grnboost2))
- **grn-network-object-handling**: Reference for handling scPRINT-derived gene networks loaded as GRnnData objects when you need differential hub detection (eigenv centrality), Louvain community detection, or prerank Enrichr enrichment on network communities. - **grn-reproducibility-overlap-assessment**: Use when benchmarking multiple GRN inference methods on single-cell (snRNAseq/scRNAseq) expression matrices and you need quantified edge reproducibility, scale-free topology fit, network characteristic metrics, and runtime per method.
